# Development log

Append dated entries in chronological order; newest entries are at the bottom.
Keep each entry brief: context, action/change or finding, then verification and limits.
Use affected-backend tags and reference a TODO task ID when applicable. Detailed
evidence stays in the linked backend report. Maintenance rules and the tag vocabulary
live in [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md#task-and-change-tracking).

This log starts on 2026-09-30; it is not a reconstruction of earlier repository history.

- Diagnosis: isolated elevated proof with exact inherited diagnostic handles reproduced child exit 0xC0000135 before Python startup, with empty stdout/stderr. PE import inspection finds this base Python's startup zlib.dll and VCRUNTIME140.dll; both are supplied by matching base (zlib also under Library/bin). Existing add_dll_directory runs too late for initial loader dependencies. The approved focused correction prepares only these finite matching-base startup DLLs, retaining provenance/pair hashes and standard CPython support without private zlib. No PATH fallback or OS change.

- Completion: `windows-symlink-verification` now passes with actual High-integrity elevation. Exactly two requested POSIX functions/imports were removed; native ACL rejection remains. Finite matching-base startup DLL preparation fixes the reproduced pre-Python loader error, with explicit source/copy equality, exact locations, optional zlib handling and fail-closed stale artifacts. Diagnostic fixture retains original deadline/identity assertions and owned handles. Final elevated focused provenance/native proof: 2 passed; full all-marker suite: 759 passed, zero skips/failures (dedicated basetemp). Ordinary Medium-token default-temp proof: 2 passed after exact elevated pytest17/18/current-link cleanup. Scoped Ruff/format/type/boundaries and package build/content/tag checks pass. ### 2026-10-03 [gui] [visual_stimulus] Right-screen derivation clarification

- Context: `gui-implementation`; owner requested removing the right-screen distance editor using the same principle as subject-to-left-wall positioning, and asked how to remove existing SpikeGLX signals.
- Review: custom SpikeGLX rows already expose × removal, while source-derived rows retain device identity and Use toggles. Independent screen placement means tank width alone is not necessarily the screen-to-screen separation. Equal wall offsets imply right distance = tank width + left-screen distance − 2 × subject-to-left-wall distance; using tank width as screen separation instead implies right distance = tank width − left-screen distance. These produce different physical projections.
- Status: asked the owner which physical constraint applies under GOV-001 before changing geometry or JSON interpretation. Initial commentary proposed equal offsets; this remains an unaccepted assumption, not an implementation/architecture decision. No code changed or tests rerun for this clarification; existing GUI remains available.

- Changes: only Left/Front/Bottom screen-distance editors remain in a row matching subject-to-wall fields. Shared geometry resolution derives right = width + left distance − 2 × subject-left-wall distance, clearing invalid/missing derived values. Both diagram and projection builder use the result. Calibration JSON v2 stores independent fields only; v1 explicit right distances must match the formula before the document can load, otherwise all existing values remain untouched.
